- **candor-gate**: candor-gate — the good fence that makes good neighbors: a two-faced discipline for skills imported from another author's garden (e.g. Bryant's Gauntlet). The CANDOR face is the relational reflex worth keeping — ask first; answer a "prove it" with the proof, not a bristle; park rather than argue; name the opening as eagerly as the hazard; earn the right to say "cut that out" and be thanked for it.
